股價相對大盤走勢

南亞科 (2408 TT)  Buy

獲利處於上行，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ： AI 運算 (GPU 、 TPU、 CPU 及 ASIC) 主導整體 DRAM
需求強勁成長，全球記憶體產業景氣循環邁入長期結構性擴張。
AI 應用領域持續擴大，推升更高容量、更高頻寬及客製化記憶
體架構需求，需求已由核心之 HBM 與 LPDDR5 全面擴展至網
路、儲存及系統管理等應用，而供應商將產能部署於 HBM 與先
進製程，對常規 DRAM 造成實質產能排擠。南亞科目前簽署長
期供應協議 (LTA) 的產能約占總出貨量的 50~70% 。不過南亞科
位元出貨量已達高點，現有廠區在 4Q27 新廠量產前產能維持
滿載，出貨量維持平穩走勢，加上公司庫存處於低檔，預估南
亞科 2027 年 bit shipment 持平，預估南亞科 2027 年稅後 EPS 
127.64 元。主要 DRAM 大廠將產能重心轉向 HBM 與 DDR5 等
高階產品，壓縮 DDR4 供給，使南亞科逐步成為 DDR4 的最大
供應商，議價能力持續提升，建議南亞科 Buy，目標價 635 元。 
3Q26 稅後 EPS 25.40 元：受惠 AI 基礎設施拉貨與長約效益浮
現，3Q26 營收為 1,336.49 億元，QoQ+61.90% ，ASP QoQ>+60% 。
匯率對營收影響 QoQ+1~3% ， bit shipment QoQ-4~-6% 。毛利率
受 Blend ASP 上升， 3Q26 毛利率上揚至 85.66% ，增加 6.17 個
百分點。南亞科 3Q26 稅後 EPS 25.40 元，淨值 118.99 元。  
預計 2026 年資本支出 697 億元 ：南亞科董事會決議為加速 5A
新廠建置並提前支付設備預付款，將 2026 年資本支出預算由原
訂的新台幣 520 億元，大幅上調至不超過新台幣 697 億元，增
加 177 億元，增幅約 34% ，較 2025 年資本支出新台幣 134 億
元，YoY 增加 420.15% ，資本支出調升主要係於 5A 新廠建置與
EUV 先進設備預付款。資本支出中 50% 用在新廠的廠務和無塵
室等，其餘 50% 則會使用在設備採購上。 5A 新廠第一階段月產
能規劃為 35.9K 片，預計於 1Q27 進行設備移入裝機， 4Q27 開
始投產貢獻出貨，目標於 2028 年底前達成每月投片量 30K 片，
並於 2030 年底前達成全產能每月投片量 50K 片之長期目標。

2 群益投顧 
  南亞科 ESG ：  
佈局綠色科技生產  
DRAM 為智慧世代的關鍵零組件，南亞科研發先進且具高效能的 「環境友善
產品」以符合市場與客戶需求。所有產品於設計初期時，即導入生命週期思
維與綠色設計，透過綠色產品推動委員會進行綠色產品管理。

重視人才發展與培養  
「人才」是公司創新、營運與永續發展的根本，強化公司全方位人才培育與
發展，南亞科技與長庚大學簽署成立記憶體學程碩士班，學生除可獲高額獎
學金，更可獲得畢業後的就業機會。南亞科技期盼從育才、用才、引才、聚
才、留才各方面，強化臺灣記憶體產業在國際上的競爭力。

共同攜手永續夥伴  
南亞科攜手上下游合作夥伴一同前行， 295 家供應商認同公司的永續理念，
一同簽署南亞科技企業社會責任承諾書且簽署率達 100% 、輔導供應商進行
永續改善，稽核、輔導與改善率均 100% 完成，並持續辦理供應鏈研討會。
此外，也與供應商共同合作推動人權及環境相關的議題。

永續績效備受肯定  
南亞科視 ESG 為企業的重要承諾，南亞科追求經營績效的同時，也同步落
實 ESG 的平衡發展。南亞科技參加 2025 第 18 屆台灣企業永續獎評選，公
司已連續第九年獲頒多項殊榮，獲獎包括年度最佳報告 (製造業 )、台灣 100
大永續典範企業獎及四項永續單項績效  – 氣候領袖獎、創新成長領袖獎、
資訊安全領袖獎與透明誠信領袖獎。  
南亞科已連續第五年入選 DJSI 世界指數，並連續第七年入選 S&P 全球永續
年鑑，持續獲得國際永續評鑑機構肯定。  
ASP 大幅增加，南亞科 3Q26 稅後 EPS 25.40 元，淨值 118.99 元：  
受惠長期合約 (LTA) 重新簽訂帶動 ASP 跳升與 AI 運算拉貨動能增溫，南亞
科 3Q26 營業收入達新台幣 1,336.49 億元， QoQ+61.90% ， YoY+611.70% 。
受長期合約重新議價影響，平均銷售單價 (ASP)QoQ>+60% ，YoY>500% ，bit 
shipment( 位元出貨量 )則因產能受限而 QoQ-4~-6% ，匯率變動則產生 1~3%
之有利影響。  
長約到期的重新簽約為近期單價上漲的主要動力 。 3Q26 平均售價 (ASP) 季增
超過 60% ，其中超過一半的漲幅來自於半年前至一年前簽訂的高涵蓋率長
約到期並重新定價。  
終端應用結構方面， AI 基礎設施 (含伺服器 )營收貢獻顯著攀升，佔比已正式
突破 20%，其餘出貨則涵蓋儲存 SSD、網通、車用工控與消費性電子等多元

南亞科董事會決議將 2026 年資本支出預算由原訂的新台幣 520 億元，提高
至不超過新台幣 697 億元：  
08/05/2026 南亞科董事會決議為加速 5A 新廠建置並提前支付設備預付款，
將 2026 年資本支出預算由原訂的新台幣 520 億元，大幅上調至不超過新台
幣 697 億元，增加 177 億元，增幅約 34% ，較 2025 年資本支出新台幣 134
億元， YoY 增加 420.15% ，資本支出調升主要係於 5A 新廠建置與 EUV 先
進設備預付款。  
資本支出中 50% 用在新廠的廠務和無塵室等，其餘 50% 則會使用在設備採
購上。累計 1Q26 至 3Q26 資本支出已執行新台幣 276 億元。預估 2026 年折
舊費用將較 2025 年減少 10% 左右，等到新廠 2027 年量產後，折舊費用才
會再增加。在位元出貨方面，現有廠區在 4Q27 新廠量產前產能維持滿載，
出貨量維持平穩走勢，加上公司庫存處於低檔，預估南亞科 2026 年 bit 
shipment 約 YoY+17~19% 。  
受惠長期合約 LTA 承諾與 AI 專案長期訂單能見度，董事會亦通過 2026-
2029 年 4 年不超過 3,466 億元的資本支出上限，全力建設 5A 新廠， 5A 新
廠全期規劃月產能達 50K 片，將導入 10nm(1B-1E) 先進製程及 EUV 技術。

4 群益投顧 
5A 新廠第一階段月產能規劃為 35.9K 片，預計於 1Q27 進行設備移入裝機，
4Q27 開始投產貢獻出貨，目標於 2028 年底前達成每月投片量 30K 片，並
於 2030 年底前達成全產能每月投片量 50K 片之長期目標。  
此外，公司已規劃於屏東科學園區建置 3D 晶圓封裝與測試廠，全面支援先
進記憶體架構封裝需求。屏東廠長期總資本支出估計高達 100 億美元，預計
於 2H28 開始進行設備移入與裝機。南亞科 (前段晶圓級 )主導前段的晶圓級
處理 (Wafer-level processing) 與晶圓級測試 (Wafer-level testing) 。由合作夥伴
福懋科負責後續的晶圓研磨、切割、打線、晶片封裝與最終測試。  
受惠技術自主研發與客製化合作深化，南亞科藉由靈活之產能配置積極應
對市場規格演進。產品布局上維持高度多樣化策略，同時供貨 DDR5 、
LPDDR5/5X 、DDR4、LPDDR4/4X 及 DDR3 系列，針對常規應用與利基型規
格動態調配投片比例，並透過 LTA 長約機制鎖定產能與合理利潤，有效抵
禦成熟規格現貨報價波動之潛在風險。  
1B/1C/1D 和 EUV 進度  
南亞科的 10nm 製程分為 A、 B、 C、 D、 E 世代，其記憶儲存細胞 (Cell) 可進
行微縮，因此可由 1A 逐步推進至 1B 再到 1C。製程轉換照計劃進行，會視
整體景氣動態調整。 1B 製程已滿載且良率高， 2026 年產出目標占比達 40%。
1C 製程預計於 1Q27 正式交付客戶進行驗證，次世代 1D、1E 製程及極紫外
光 (EUV) 微影技術導入開發進度亦完全符合內部規劃。現有廠區在既有空間
內持續優化先進節點投片轉換，以確保 DDR5 與 LPDDR5 等高階規格產出
持續攀升；  
EUV 在 1C 和 1D 製程都有導入，甚至 1E，還是要看客戶需求，目前預期
2028 年導入採用；在興建新廠時亦會同步建置 EUV 極紫外光微影設備獨立
廠房。 1C/1D/1E 及 EUV 研發也依計畫推進。  
4Q26 成長來自價格向上，預估南亞科 4Q26 稅後 EPS 28.94 元：  
DRAM 是算力的關鍵零組件，在 AI 驅動的投資下，推動記憶體供需結構性
改變，降低景氣循環因素。 HBM、LPDDR5 與 RDIMM 需求強勁。 HBM、高
容量 DDR5 及 LPDDR5 是整體 DRAM 需求成長的主要動能，高階 e-SSD 、
BMC 、 NIC 對 DDR4 、 LPDDR4 及 DDR5 需求持續。  
一般型 DRAM 方面，高階 PC 與手機對 DDR5 及 LPDDR5 需求維持穩定；
其他消費性方面，高價消費型產品需求暢旺，低價產品需求放緩，整體而言，
AI 需求強排擠手機、 PC、車用及消費型記憶體供給，預計記憶體供給不足
預期將持續數季以上，長期 LTA ( 數年合約 )形成供需共識。  
AI 伺服器與高效能運算拉貨動能外溢，加上手機與一般運算記憶體供給端
受限， 4Q26 DRAM 產業結構性缺貨態勢維持不變。原廠庫存普遍偏低，且
供應商均依據 LTA 長約承諾進行排產，新廠建置、設備交期與新製程導入
均需要較長前置時間，整體供給彈性極為有限，供需緊俏格局為 4Q26 營運
提供基本面支撐。 4Q26 報價已確定高於 3Q26 ，南亞科多數產品合約價持續
上揚，公司對整體價格走勢樂觀，且長約占比持續提高，短單價格仍可能因
市況波動而調整。南亞科 2H26 成長主要來自 ASP，因為庫存在低點，產能
利用率已滿載，預估南亞科 4Q26 稅後 EPS 28.94 元。預估 2026 年稅後 EPS

5 群益投顧 
76.44 元。  
產能受限，預估南亞科 2027 年稅後 EPS 127.64 元：  
AI 運算 (GPU 、 TPU、 CPU 及 ASIC) 主導整體 DRAM 需求強勁成長，全球記
憶體產業景氣循環邁入長期結構性擴張。 AI 應用領域持續擴大，推升更高
容量 、 更高頻寬及客製化記憶體架構需求 ， 需求已由核心之HBM 與 LPDDR5
全面擴展至網路、儲存及系統管理等應用，而供應商將產能部署於 HBM 與
先進製程，對常規 DRAM 造成實質產能排擠，使 DRAM 供需缺口將長期持
續 ， 關鍵觀察指標為北美CSP 及大型語言模型開發商的 AI 投資回報率 (ROI)
是否可持續。  
傳統應用市場在供給不足與價格調漲環境下，傳統應用產品出現規格下調
(down-specification) 或延長生命週期的狀況，低階市場部分由二手或回收
DRAM 支撐。  
DDR4 走勢：  
 供給短缺：由於主要供應商減產並將產能轉移至  HBM/DDR5 ，DDR4 市
場出現供給短缺，且此狀況預計將持續數個季度。  
 市場轉移至 DDR5 ：雖然 DDR4 市場占比逐漸變小，目前單一 DDR4 產
品仍佔整體 DRAM 市場總需求 12%~14% ， 若加計 LPDDR4 則比例更高 。
南亞科全球 DRAM 市佔率約為 3%，因此相對龐大的 DDR4 需求，為公
司提供相當充沛且健康的銷售空間。  
 需求穩固：消費性電子是 DDR4 需求最穩固的領域。 8Gb 容量是目前市
場的最佳選擇點 (sweet spot) 。每個客戶的應用中，可能有完整的基礎設
施 (平台 )，包括 PCB、晶片組等等。所以，短期對客戶來說，從 DDR4 轉
移到  DDR5 相對複雜，客戶必須在基礎設施變更上花費大量的時間和金
錢，且 16Gb DDR5 價格也上漲，成本高昂，因此需求將維持較長時間。
 南亞科 16Gb DDR4 於 2H26 開始貢獻營收。南亞科具備高度彈性，可隨
客戶需求與市場效益，在 DDR4 、 LPDDR4 、 DDR5 與 AI 專案之間彈性
調配產能。   
DDR5 ：  
 AI 運算 (GPU、TPU、CPU、ASIC) 全面驅動高容量與高頻寬記憶體需求，
加上個人電腦 (PC) 領域主要採用 DDR5 ，帶動 DDR5 需求穩健成長。  
 南亞科進度：公司已開始以模組形式出貨 DDR5 ，技術進度與市場同步，
高階的 6400 Mbps 產品已準備就緒。  
 目前 DDR5 交易量約占 10% ，公司目標是維持在 10% 以上並逐步提升，
但仍需視整體缺貨結構而定，短期內不會過度拉升產出，以避免對客戶
造成調整壓力。 DDR5 8Gb R-DIMM 主要應用於雲端與伺服器，但在既
有產能未擴增的情況下，大量出貨仍需等待新廠投產後才有空間。

Wafer-on-Wafer (WoW) 堆疊技術：

### Page 6

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 13 日

6 群益投顧 
 公司正積極開發  WoW 技術，以應對未來  AI/AGI 應用所需的高頻寬與
高密度需求。  
 目前相關設備已安裝，並正與多家客戶合作開發不同領域的終端產品，
但因保密協議無法透露具體應用。預估 2027 年有營收貢獻。  
 南亞科正與多位客戶合作開發客製化  DRAM ，其中也包含  HBM 的延
伸產品以及  Wafer-to-Wafer 封裝專案。由於具體進度取決於客戶端的
市場開發時程，目前尚無法給出明確的營收貢獻指引  
 台灣擁有領先晶圓代工廠 (如台積電 )，這的確有助於簡化並推動南亞科
在客製化專案 (如  HBM 及  Wafer-to-Wafer) 上的進行。  
南亞科的長期合約 (LTA) 佔總出貨量的涵蓋率約落在 50%~70% 。 合約期限從
單季到 3 年不等，其中 3 年期的超長約已佔出貨量  20% 以上。隨著 AI 與伺
服器客戶積極爭取長期產能保障，南亞科正持續與客戶推進更長天期的供
應合約，計畫逐步將過去短天期的 1 年期合約，往 3 年以上的「長期供應合
約」進行結構性轉換，以鎖定長期合作夥伴的份額。為應對記憶體產業的週
期性波動，南亞科正與客戶洽談導入包含 「價格底線與上限」 的合約機制 (如
保證採購、價格底線或預付款 )，在保障供應商合理利潤的同時，也為客戶
鎖定成本上限。這些長期合約不僅涵蓋現有的廠房產能，也涵蓋未來新廠的
整體產能規劃。一般來說，如果是季度合約，價格會在一個季度內維持固定，
雙方都會遵守約定，並在下一次談判週期到來前盡量不作調整。  
產業結構性轉變擴大需求缺口，加速多元創新  AI 解決方案，記憶體需求
維持熱絡， AI 相關需求降低景氣循環波動， AI GPU /CPU/ASIC 晶片開發持
續推升 HBM 、 LPDDR5 及 DDR5 需求大幅成長。 AI 基礎建設帶動  eSSD 、
SmartNIC 與 BMC 採用更高容量、更高效 DRAM ，以因應高速資料處理、網
路傳輸及系統管理需求。非 AI 應用產能受限，有利於高階市場發展，使終
端產品售價提升。  
南亞科預期供需緊缺的狀況預計將持續延續至 2027 年甚至 2028 年初。南
亞科目前的策略則是技術自主、長約鎖定與利潤導向的防禦性資本支出，而
非衝產量、殺價拼市占。南亞科目前簽署長期供應協議 (LTA) 的產能約占總
出貨量的 50~70% 。不過南亞科位元出貨量已達高點，現有廠區在 4Q27 新
廠量產前產能維持滿載，出貨量維持平穩走勢，加上公司庫存處於低檔，預
估南亞科 2027 年 bit shipment 持平，價格走勢維持高檔，使南亞科毛利率
水準有望優於 2026 年，預估南亞科 2027 年稅後 EPS 127.64 元。

  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

CHPT
Valuation
We value CHPT using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the structural shift towards high-end
testing. Our target price of NT$4,350 is derived from applying 35x to 2027E EPS, implying the company's average forward PE
since 2024 +1 SD, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the 2nm/3nm testing segment,
rising contribution from US AI GPU & AI ASIC projects, and the unique competitive advantage of its "All-in-House" vertical

integration model. With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play
(rather than a consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant system rates the CHPT stock as High Risk for its share price volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price, and to our earnings forecasts, include: 1) margin

compression from lower-than-expected utilization rates; 2) execution delays in concentrated US AI GPU and ASIC programs;
3) intensifying competition in testing boards from global peers; and 4) structural gross margin dilution from a shift towards
high-end burn-in boards.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 15 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
因 應 企 業 永 續 經 營 所 帶 來 的 挑 戰 與 機 會，整 合 各 方 利 害 關 係 人 關 注 之 議 題，
將 展 開 「 永 續 經 營(ESG) 計 畫 」 ， 成 立 企 業 永 續 發 展 委 員 會 ， 聚 焦 於 五 大 策
略 ， 連 結 聯 合 國 「 永 續 發 展 目 標 SDGs 」 ， 以 企 業 核 心 精 神 與 專 業 ， 落 實 永
續 經 營 各 項 目 標，促 進 永 續 的 經 濟 成 長、社 會 發 展 與 環 境 保 護，提 升 企 業 競
爭 力 與 發 揮 影 響 力 。 
公 司 簡 介 ： 
藥華藥主要致力於研發和生產新一代創新型生物藥，特別聚焦於血液腫瘤
領 域 。 公 司 旗 艦 產 品 為 Ropeginterferon alfa -2b ( 簡稱 Ropeg ，商品名
Besremi) ， 是 一 個 創 新 的 長 效 型 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 目 前 已 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 、
韓 國 、 台 灣 及 中 國 等 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 取 得 藥 證 ， 用 於 治 療 成 人 真 性 紅 血 球
增多症 (Polycythemia Vera, PV) ， 並 已 陸 續 於 台 灣 、 日 本 及 美 國 取 得 原 發 性
血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 藥 證 。  
Ropeg 是 藥 華 藥 自 行 研 發、並 擁 有 全 球 獨 家 權 利 的 新 藥。公 司 利 用 其 特 有 的
長 效 型 蛋 白 質 藥 物 技 術 平 台 ， 成 功 開 發 出Ropeg 。 過 去 PV 的 治 療 藥 物 有 限
且 常 伴 隨 副 作 用 ， 包 含 老 藥 HU 及 尚 未 獲 PV 適 應 症 批 准 的 干 擾 素 藥 物 ， 與
美 國 藥 廠 Incyte 開 發 的 Jakafi (ruxolitinib) 。 2019 年， Ropeg 獲 得 歐 洲 藥 品
管理局 (EMA) 核 准 上 市 ， 並 在 2021 年 11 月 獲 得 美 國 FDA 的 核 准 ， 成 為 第
一個被 FDA 批 准 用 於 所 有 PV 成 人 的 治 療 藥 物 ， 不 受 之 前 治 療 史 的 限 制 ，
也 是 第 一 個 被 FDA 核 准 特 別 用 於 PV 的 干 擾 素 療 法 ， 且 已 連 續 四 年 獲NCCN
治 療 指 引 推 薦 。 
相 較 於 傳 統 干 擾 素 ，Ropeg 具 有 更 長 的 半 衰 期 ， 因 此 可 以 延 長 給 藥 間 隔 ， 提
高 病 患 的 便 利 性 及 順 從 性，同 時 也 展 現 出 更 優 異 的 療 效。 在 7.3 年 的 臨 床 追
蹤 數 據 顯 示 76% 的 完 全 血 液 學 反 應 率(CHR) ， 並 能 持 續 降 低 JAK2 基 因 突 變
負 擔 。 藥 華 藥 在 美 國 、 歐 洲 、 日 本 及 台 灣 等 地 設 有 子 公 司 ， 負 責 藥 物 的 銷
售 與 推 廣 。Ropeg 目 前 是 公 司 最 主 要 的 營 收 來 源 ， 佔 比 超 過9 成 。 此 外 也 積
極拓展 Ropeg 的 其 他 適 應 症 ， 包 含 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 及 骨 髓 纖 維 化(MF) 等。。 
3Q26 營 收 季 增，9 月 未 含 CMS 調 整 仍 創 新 高，財 務 營 運 槓 桿 與 獲 利 結 構 持
續 優 化 ：  
2Q2 6 營收 68. 55 億元 (YoY+90.30%) ， 主 要 源 於 美 國 與 日 本 兩 大 高 價 值 市 場
銷 量 顯 著 成 長 ， 團 隊 擴 編 與 精 準 醫 學 行 銷 策 略 效 益 大 舉 顯 現 。 毛 利 率
94.2 3%， 受 惠 美 國 5M2 6 調 升 藥 價，以及 CMS 回沖， 營 業 費 用 33.00 億元
(YoY +60.68%) ， 增 加 主 因 延 續 團 隊 擴 編 、 商 業 推 廣 以 及 依 會 計 準 則 認 列 限
制 員 工 權 利 新 股 約 2 億多元 ，增 幅 遠 低 於 營 收 成 長，展 現 高 營 運 槓 桿 之 強 勁
爆發力 。  
ET 新 適 應 症 在 全 球 步 入 收 割 期 ， 併 購 予 宇 切 入 新 興 療 法 ， 加 上PMF 收案
順 利 與 肝 癌 術 後 100% 防 復 發 數 據 ， 中 長 期 佈 局 完 整：  
原 發 性 血 小 板 過 多 症(ET) 為過去 30 年 來 缺 乏 新 藥 之 領 域 ， 公 司 於05/12/2026
率 先 取 得 台 灣 ET 藥 證 ， 用 於 HU 抗 藥 性 或 無 法 耐 受 之 成 人 ET 病 人 ； 日 本
MHLW 專 家 會 議 於 07/2026 通 過 審 查 ， 破 例 同 意 將 全 球 三 期 SURPASS -ET
與 北 美 單 臂 二 期 XC 數 據 合 併 評 估 ， 並 已 於 08/24/2026 正 式 獲 日 本 核 准 用

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 15 日

3 群益投顧 
於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 包 括 未 曾 接 受 治 療 者 ； 隨 後 美 國FDA 亦 正 式 核 准 Ropeg
用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 ， 成 為 近 30 年首款 ET 新 藥 ， 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔
期，配 合 芝 加 哥 誓 師 大 會 已 完 成 之 團 隊 培 訓 與 推 廣 文 宣，取 證 後 已 立 即 展 開
商 業 推 廣 ； 其 他 市 場 方 面 ， 中 國 大 陸 與 韓 國ET 審 查 同 步 進 行 ， 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 及 12/2026 取 證 ， 同 時 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申
請。早 期 原 發 性 骨 髓 纖 維 化(PMF) 三 期 臨 床 HOPE -PMF 於 06/2026 完 成 收 案
且 超 收 至 160 人 (原目標 150 人 )， 預 計 2027 年 完 成 試 驗 、2028 年 中 申 請 藥
證。  
在 實 體 腫 瘤 領 域 ，B 型 肝 炎 相 關 肝 癌(HCC) 術 後 防 復 發 一 期 試 驗 ，Ropeg 序
列合併 Anti -PD1 在 15 名 患 者 中 位 追 蹤 1,024 天 ， 創 下 100% 無 復 發 存 活 率
(RFS) 之 重 大 突 破 。PEG -IL -2 (P11838) 、 PD -1-IL -2v 、 ADC 及 KRAS TCR -T
將於 2026 -2027 年 陸 續 申 請 IND 。  
藥 華 藥 近 期 斥 資 約 1.68 億 元 收 購 予 宇 生 技 100% 股 權 ， 取 得 脂 質 奈 米 顆 粒
(LNP) 藥 物 遞 送 平 台 、 研 發 設 備 及 專 業 團 隊 ， 並 納 入 兩 項 新 藥 研 發 專 案 。 予
宇成立於 2019 年， LNP 平 台 涵 蓋 配 方 設 計 、 微 流 體 製 程 與 分 析 方 法 ， 可 支
援 mRNA 、 saRNA 及 circRNA 等 多 元 RNA 藥 物 開 發 ； 原 有 FR -202 與 FR -
201 兩 項 專 案 分 別 鎖 定 端 粒 生 物 學 疾 病 與 威 爾 森 氏 症 ， 預 計 於 2027 年及
2028 年 提 出 IND 。  
此 項 收 購 對 公 司 營 運 具 三 大 正 面 意 義 ：(1) 收 購 金 額 僅 約 1.68 億 元 ， 相 對 公
司單季逾 25 億 元 淨 利 規 模 ， 財 務 負 擔 極 低 ；(2) 取 得 遞 送 系 統 自 主 能 力 ， 可
與公司既有蛋白質藥物製造資源整合，從研發早期即同步規劃藥物設計與
後 續 製 造 ；(3) 延 伸 研 發 版 圖 至 In Vivo CAR -T 此 一 國 際 大 廠 積 極 布 局 的 領
域 ， 提 升 中 長 期 選 擇 權 價 值 ， 亦 為 後 續 研 發 產 品 線 布 局 增 加 深 度 與 廣 度 。 
注 射 筆 上 市 與 潛 力 患 者 數 上 修 雙 輪 驅 動，配 合 真 實 世 界 數 據 防止 惡 化，鞏 固
Ropeg 在 PV 領 域 之 地 位 ：  
Ropeg 在 PV 適 應 症 已 於 全 球 約 50 個 國 家 獲 批 ， 加 拿 大 亦 於 07/02/2026 
順 利 取 證 。 美 國 FDA 於 06/2026 核准 Ropeg 注 射 筆 ， 並 於 07/2026 正 式 出
貨 開 賣，注 射 筆 具 備 極 細 針 頭、精 準 調 劑 及 一 次 性 自 動 縮 回 防 針 扎 設 計，短
期 可 加 速 替 換 傳 統 預 充 充 填 針 筒 Prefilled Syringe ， 中 長 期 將 提 升 患 者 長 期
用 藥 依 從 性 與 留 存 率 。 
武田製藥 (Takeda) 競品 Rusfertide (擬 似 鐵 調 素)預 計 於 08/2026 獲 批，藥 華 藥
指出  Rusfertide 僅 定 位 於 配 合 放 血 之 被 動 症 狀 控 制 ， 而 Ropeg 為 一 線 首 選
積 極 療 法 ， 真 實 世 界 數 據 證 實 Ropeg 合 併 治 療 可 使 患 者 疾 病 惡 化成 骨 髓 纖
維化  (MF )的 概 率 維 持 在 0%。最 新 流 行 病 學 文 獻 將 美 國 MPN 總 潛 力 患 者 數
上修至 34.70 萬人 ( 原 30.50 萬人 )， 競 品 進 入市 場 將 大 幅 提 升 早 期 治 療 之 病
識 感 與 診 斷 率 ， 反 而 有 助 於 Ropeg 進 一 步 擴 大 一 線 市 場 壟 斷 地 位 。 
藥 華 藥 與 AOP 仲 裁 案 ： 
關於 AOP 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 ， 藥 華 藥 認 為 其 程 序 存 在 違 反 程 序 正 義 及 聽 審
權 之 重 大 瑕 疵 ， 目 前 正 與 外 部 律 師 團 隊 嚴 謹 評 估 ， 將 在 法 定 時 限 內 向 德 國
法 蘭 克 福 高 等 法 院 提 起 「 撤 銷 第 二 階 段 仲 裁 判 斷 之 訴 」 。 
由 於 法 律 程 序 仍 在 評 估 中 ， 現 階 段 無 法 判 斷 或 預 測 該 裁 定 金 額 發 生 的 時 間
點 ， 後 續 發 展 視 訴 訟 進 度 而 定 。 公 司 與AOP 之 合 約 目 前 仍 處 於 有 效 狀 態 ，

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 15 日

4 群益投顧 
對 於 任 何 有 助 於 妥 善 解 決 爭 議 、 維 護 股 東 權 益 之 方 案 均 持 開 放 態 度 。 歐 洲
仍 為 藥 華 藥 極 為 重 視 之 核 心 市 場 ， 將 綜 合 評 估 法 規 要 求 、 商 業 推 廣 與 契 約
關 係 ， 持 續 規 劃 並 評 估 於 歐 洲 申 請 ET 藥 證 之 可 行 性 。 
AOP 在 8/7/2026 發 布 的 官 方 新 聞 稿 表 示 ， 藥 華 藥 延 遲 造 成 的 損 害 約€82M ，
2019 -2022 過 高 價 格 所 產 生 的 退 款 約 €31M ， AOP 原 本 應 支 付 給 藥 華 藥 的
profit sharing 約 17M 可 用 來 抵 銷 ； 最 終 AOP 表 示 自 己 不 用 付 款 給 藥 華 藥 ，
並 認 為 藥 華 藥 目 前 對 AOP 的 責 任 已 進 入 「 三 位 數 百 萬 歐 元 」 範 圍 。 推 論 藥
華 藥 實 際 曝 險 金 額 Net Set -off 後 約 為 €97.48M (約新台幣 36 億元 )。  
AOP 提 出 延 伸 主 張 ， 若 將 仲 裁 庭 對 2019 -2022 年 價 格 爭 議( 最高 900% 超額
定 價 之 認 定)的 計 算 邏 輯 套 用 至 2023 -2025 年供貨， AOP 主 張 藥 華 藥 需 再 退
還約 €65M ， 此 項 目 僅 為 AOP 單 方 面 之 延 伸 主 張 ， 非 現 階 段 確 定 之 仲 裁 判
決。  
藥 華 藥 已 從 純 研 發 型 生 技 公 司 轉 型 為 具 備 強 勁 商 業 化 現 金 流 之 藥 廠 ， 一 次
性認列約 36 億 元 損 失 衝 擊 ， 不 至 於 構 成 營 運 生 存 危 機。

投 資 建 議：(1)07/2026 於 美 國 開 賣 Ropeg 注 射 筆 提 升 患 者 用 藥 順 應 性 與 留 存
率 ， 推 升 低 風 險 PV/ET 患 者 滲 透 率 ， 為 2H26 營 收 加 速 引 擎 ；(2)ET 新 適 應
症 迎 來 多 國 藥 證 收 割，開 啟 第 二 成 長 曲 線，台 灣、日本 取 證 後，美國 FDA 亦
核 准 用 於 所 有 成 人 ET 患 者 並 具 7 年 孤 兒 藥 獨 佔 期，中 國 與 韓 國 分 別 預 計 於
11/2026 與 12/2026 取 證 ， 並 準 備 於 澳 洲 啟 動 PV 及 ET 雙 適 應 症 藥 證 申 請。
(3) 預估 2026 年 EPS 2 4.93 元、 2027 年 EPS 42. 45 元 ， 投 資 建 議 買 進 ， 目 標
價 1,6 00 元 。

7 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

百和(9938) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：最壞已過且股價低於淨值，靜待基本面起飛

一、3Q26 自結 EPS 0.62 元，低於預期；主要受到網
布毛利率下滑且業外利息費用所致 
百和 3Q26 營收 44.09 億元(+15.9% QoQ / +5.8% YOY)，
就各產品比重來看，黏扣帶 12.3%、織鞋帶 44.1%、鬆
緊帶 13%、射出勾 6.6%、針織經編提花網布 20.1%以
及其他 3.9%。3Q26 就各產品線來看，所有產品線均成
長，僅有經編網布衰退，其中鬆緊帶+16%YoY、黏扣帶
+9.7%YoY、織鞋帶+9% YOY、射出勾+6.7%YoY 以及
針織經編提花網布-8.4% YOY。獲利方面，3Q26 毛利
率 29.5%，與 2Q26 約略持平，也較3Q25 下滑 6.3%，
主要反映關稅影響、產品組合以及受到美伊戰爭影響原
物料成本上揚所致；就 3Q26 各產品線毛利率來看，粘
扣帶 27.6%、 鬆緊帶37.9%、 射出勾44%、 織鞋帶30.4%、
經編網布 17.2％以及其他 37.5%。其中經編網布毛利率
較 2Q26 回升 2.7%，但仍低於 2025 年水準，主要反映
產品組合差異以及稼動率的影響；3Q26 合併營業利益
達 4.35 億元，營業利益率 9.9%，業外淨損 1.41 億元，
主要反映百和-KY 利息費用，稅前淨利 2.94 億元，稅後
歸屬母公司淨利 1.84 億元，EPS 0.62 元。  
二 、 考量原物料成本提升且通膨持續影響全球消費景氣 ，
下修百和 2026 年營收為低個位數增長且因毛利率表現
低於預期，下修 2026 年 EPS 為 2.48 元 
9M26 前十大客戶佔比 65.5%，德國品牌仍為第一大客
戶 ， 佔比25.1%，YOY-5%、 美系品牌15.7%，YOY+2%，
其中鞋子持平、服飾類+11%、瑞士品牌為第三大客戶，
佔營收比重 6.1%，YOY-11%，New Balance 占比 4.4%，
YOY-11%。HOKA 已為前十大客戶 ， 佔營收比重2.3%，
YOY+58%，為前九月成長性最佳客戶。就產品應用別
來看 ，前九月Footwear 占比 60.4%、Apparel 25.3%、
3C 2.8%； 以前九個月YOY 來看 ，Footwear-1% YOY、
Apparel +12% YOY、3C 產品+16% YOY 以及醫療-3% 
YOY，但單就第三季表現來看，Footwear 持平、
Apparel+27% YOY、3C+20% YOY 以及醫療+31% 
YOY，顯示其他應用端需求起來，未來百和將持續著重
其他類別發展，不單僅限於鞋子跟衣服，醫療跟 3C 已
漸有成效。過去 3C 是客人指定材料，百和僅有加工；
但現在幾乎 80%都是百和的材料進行加工，做成客戶想
要的東西，附加價值較高。3C 及醫療目前營收占比約
6%，未來可望緩步增加。 
考量美伊戰爭引發原物料成本提升且戰爭仍未平息影響
全球消費景氣，輔以客戶下單仍顯保守；我們預估百和
2026 年營收為 161.25 億元 (+2.2% YoY)，其中傳產中
個位數成長、網布中~高個位數衰退；毛利率方面，考量
原物料價格上揚因素未解、關稅影響持續且經編網布毛
利率低於預期 ， 保守預估2026 年集團毛利率為 30.7%，
較 2025 年下滑 4.6%，下修本業獲利為 17.68 億元，營
業利益率 11%，業外利息費用與 2025 年大致相當，預
估稅後歸屬母公司淨利潤 7.41 億元 ， 下修EPS 為 2.48
元。 
資本支出方面，2025 年實際資本支出 19.01 億元，其中
廠房建物 26％、設備購置 72%以及其他 2%。9M26 資
本支出 22.69 億元，其中台灣百和3.31 億元、百和興業
19.38 億元；2026 年預期資本支出約 28.02 億元，主要
以百和興業為主，約占 3/4；台灣百和約 1/4，主要用在
機器設備。 
三、下修經編緹花網布 2026 年至中~高個位數衰退，長
期仍可望有轉單效益 
2025 年隨著品牌採用意願提升，網布營收貢獻達 34.08
億元，YOY+38%，毛利率 34.09%；2026 年原預估在
新產能以及核心客戶、新興客戶放量且打入成衣供應鏈
帶動，成長性不亞於 2025 年，但受到全球大環境不確
定性高且受到產品組合以及主要客戶新一代跑鞋主料轉
用梭織且非由百和-KY 供應影響，前九個月網布營收衰
4.9%，我們下修 2026 年經編緹花網布營收為 31.46 億
元，-7.68% YOY， 毛利率受到稼動率偏低且產品組合影
響，前三季僅 19.9%。現在網布往圓編、橫編等更多織
法開發，未來可望持續擴大應用面，未來隨著應用面擴
大且稼動率提升，毛利率有機會往 2025 年水準邁進。 
網布客戶占比來看 ，2025 年德系品牌仍是第一大客戶，

四、維持買進理由 
2026 年受到美伊戰爭引發原物料成本提升且戰爭仍未
平息影響全球消費景氣，輔以客戶下單仍顯保守；我們
預估百和 2026 年營收為 161.25 億元 (+2.2% YoY)，其
中傳產中個位數成長 、 網布中~高個位數衰退 ， 下修EPS
為 2.48 元。展望 2027 年，隨著品牌對應庫存調整到較
健康的水位，下單可望回到正常模式。大環境穩定後，
原物料成本可以趨於正常、關稅議題減緩，2027 年可望
優於 2026 年，預估 2027 年 EPS 3.23 元。股價方面，
考量最壞已過 、2027 年可望恢復成長且目前股價低於淨
值，故維持買進評等，目標價 48 元(15X 2027PER)。

제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

Document 244: Anthropic招股书“刺激了贪婪”！CoreWeave领衔AI交易反弹

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic招股书“刺激了贪婪”！CoreWeave领衔AI交易反弹.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Anthropic 招股书披露上半年营收 163 亿美元、同⽐暴增近 14 倍，打消了市场对 AI 烧钱不可
持续的担忧。 CoreWeave 单⽇涨 8% 领衔 AI 基础设施板块全线反弹。 Anthropic 对算⼒的⻓
期资本承诺，直接验证了新云服务商、存储及光学连接等基础设施赛道的需求前景。
摘要：
Anthropic 周⼀提交 IPO 申请，招股书显⽰其今年上半年营收达 163 亿美元，同⽐增⻓近 14 倍。这
⼀数字远超部分投资者此前的悲观预期，令华尔街情绪迅速逆转。
周⼆，纳斯达克综合指数收涨 1.2% ，延续周⼀ 0.8% 的涨幅。 AI 云计算、存储、光学连接等基础
设施板块全线⾛⾼，此前因利率担忧和宏观压⼒遭受重挫的个股领涨。
CoreWeave 周⼆⼤涨 8% ， Nebius Group 涨幅更达 11.2% ，成为当⽇最亮眼的两只 AI 基础设施
股。
存储板块同步受益。西部数据涨 7% ，希捷科技涨 3.7% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

此外，戴尔周⼆收涨 7.5% ，今年以来该股已因 AI 服务器热潮⼤幅⾛⾼。光学连接股 Coherent 涨
6.4% ， Lumentum 涨 5.9% 。 AI 连接芯⽚供应商 Credo 和 Astera Labs 分别⼤涨 10.8% 和 10.5% 。
Anthropic 对算⼒的⻓期资本承诺，直接验证了新云服务商、存储及光学连接等基础设施赛道的
需求前景。值得注意的是，这波反弹发⽣在埃隆 · ⻢斯克周末公开唱空的背景下 —— ⻢斯克预⾔
新⼀代云服务商将⾯临资本枯竭。他称， 200 多家新型云服务商将⾯临整合，有些公司能够挺过
去，但数量不会太多。市场显然选择⽤脚投票，⽆视了这⼀警告。
招股书数据 “ 令⼈震惊 ” ， IPO 或严重超额认购
InvestorPlace 分析师 Louis Navellier 在给客⼾的报告中写道， Anthropic 的 S-1 招股书 " 正在引发
⼤量兴奋情绪 " 。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
他直接点出了核⼼数据：上半年营收 163 亿美元，同⽐增⻓近 14 倍。
尽管公司仍处于运营亏损状态， Navellier 认为这种 " 令⼈震惊 " 的营收增速将推动 IPO" 严重超额认
购 "—— 即市场对股票的需求将远超实际发⾏量。
⻓期资本承诺，是基础设施股上涨的真正逻辑
Tortoise Capital ⾼级组合经理 Rob Thummel 向 MarketWatch 解释了这波⾏情背后的投资逻辑。
"Anthropic 对资本⽀出做出了⻓期的重⼤承诺， " 他说。随着算⼒需求持续攀升，⽀撑这些部署
的基础设施需求也将同步增⻓ —— 这直接利好向客⼾出租算⼒的新云服务商。
Thummel 还特别提到 Anthropic 为锁定未来算⼒⽽签订的⼀系列合同，称这反映出市场对 AI 基础

设施需求 " 将在相当⻓时间内持续强劲 " 的信⼼。
Lazard ：市场对这类资产仍有充裕胃⼝
Lazard Asset Management 组合经理 Celine Woo 向 MarketWatch 表⽰，尽管 Anthropic IPO 的定
价和需求尚不明朗，但这份招股书本⾝⽆疑是⼀个看涨信号。
" 从投资者⻆度来看，市场对持有这类商业模式的资产仍有充裕的胃⼝， " 她说。
Anthropic 的商业模式 —— 以及其竞争对⼿的模式 —— 都需要⼤量芯⽚和算⼒来维持运转。这⼀
逻辑正在为整条 AI 供应链提供⽀撑。
芯⽚巨头财报助攻，⼤科技公司烧钱不⽌
Morningstar ⾸席欧洲股票策略师 Michael Field 在周⼀的报告中指出， ASML （涨 2.9% ）和台积
电（涨 3% ）均因先进芯⽚技术需求旺盛⽽超预期，并对⾏业前景给出了 " 乐观展望 " 。
但⼤科技公司的 AI ⽀出同样没有放缓迹象。 Meta 上周发布强劲财报后，周⼀宣布计划通过股权
融资筹集最⾼ 950 亿美元。 Alphabet 、微软和亚⻢逊则将于本周晚些时候相继公布财报。

Document 272: Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

### Page 3

Related articles
risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

Global air travel growth is set to rebound to 5% in 2027, but
airlines face tougher conditions. Demand remains resilient, but
the fuel price shock will spill over into 2027, while new aircraft
supply remains constrained. This creates divergent fortunes
across airlines, segments and regions
Rico Luman
The aviation industry now faces a growing challenge to deliver proﬁtable
growth as capacity remains constrained, costs rise, and geopolitical risks
intensify

The fuel-price shock will continue into 2027. Jet fuel
could account for close to 35% of airlines’ operating
costs in 2026, up from 27% in 2025. Tight reﬁnery
capacity and low inventories are likely to keep prices
elevated even if oil prices ease.
European air fares will have further to rise. Hedging
initially protected many European airlines, but that cover
will progressively expire during 2027. US fares have

Budget airlines are particularly exposed. Fuel represents

Premium carriers have more scope to raise fares without

Aircraft shortages will continue to constrain capacity.
Airbus and Boeing had a combined backlog of around

values and leasing rates high and forcing airlines to
operate older, less fuel-eﬃcient planes for longer.
Asia is becoming the industry’s main long-term growth

of the global aircraft ﬂeet is expected to rise from roughly
one-third to around 45%, with India standing out: its
ﬂeet could grow by approximately 8% a year over the
next two decades.

Air traﬃc to and from the Middle East has taken a signiﬁcant hit as

a result of the war, which has led to airspace closures and
passengers shifting to other destinations due to safety concerns.
And this comes on top of the Russian airspace, which complicates
operations. Traﬃc in this pivotal region on the East-West corridor
halved in March and April and was still 15% lower year-on-year in
August 2026. Before the war, the Middle East accounted for 9.5% of
global revenue passenger kilometres (RPKs). On balance, the

setback in the region shaved some 2-3 percentage points oﬀ global
growth with additional impacts elsewhere.

Jet fuel continues to trade high in Q4 2026
European jet fuel prices in $ per tonne (ARA)
Source: Reﬁnitiv, ING Research
Cost shock hit proﬁtability, but for some
airlines more than others

Jet fuel is often the largest or second-largest cost item for airlines.
In 2025, it accounted for 27% of total operational costs on average.
This share could move towards 35% in 2026 as a result of
persistently high jet fuel prices and elevated crack spreads, caused
by the loss of Middle Eastern supplies and tight global availability.
Further geopolitical developments remain highly uncertain. Even if
the Middle East conﬂict is resolved and oil prices retreat, tight

reﬁnery capacity and depleted inventories are likely to keep middle-
distillate prices, including jet fuel, elevated well into 2027.
High fuel prices impact proﬁts in US and
Europe, low cost carriers most vulnerable

### Page 7

Operational proﬁt (EBIT) in % per peiod
Source: Company reports, ING Research
Fuel hedging provides less cushioning in
2027; fares must respond
Airlines employ very diﬀerent strategies to protect themselves
against fuel price shocks, using derivatives based on crude oil or jet
fuel. European airlines generally hedged 40-90% of their jet fuel
consumption, cushioning the initial impact of the price shock.
However, the Middle East conﬂict has lasted too long to avoid a

signiﬁcant impact. Existing hedges gradually roll oﬀ, increasing
exposure to prevailing market prices. Ryanair, which remains (80%)
hedged until the second quarter of 2027, has already warned of
signiﬁcantly higher fares.
Outside, the picture is mixed, but hedge ratios are generally lower,
and airlines in the US and China, for instance, don’t hedge at all.
This means that two airlines operating similar routes can face very
diﬀerent fuel bills.
Airline tickets in the US responded much

faster to the cost shock than in Europe

Index ticket prices vs consumer price index (CPI, seasonally adjusted) in

Fares already sharply higher in the US, less
so in Europe
The fuel price shock will increasingly feed through into higher fares.
US airlines’ practice of retaining relatively high exposure to fuel price
volatility resulted in an almost immediate pass-through, with ticket
prices rising solidly in the months after jet fuel prices started to rise.

It is also easier to raise prices when domestic competitors face a
similar cost shock.
The situation is diﬀerent in Europe, where hedging provided more
initial protection. This also means fares may still need to rise
signiﬁcantly if fuel prices fail to fall. Coverage gradually expires as
2027 progresses, leaving airlines increasingly exposed to higher
spot prices.

proﬁtable

More factors are driving up costs,
particularly in Europe
Jet fuel is the clear outlier this year, but wage and maintenance
costs are also rising faster than the average price level. Borrowing
costs and aircraft leasing rates remain another factor, although the
impact depends on individual airlines’ strategies and ﬁnancial
structures.
European airlines also face rising costs from the European Union
Emissions Trading System (ETS) for ﬂights within the European

Economic Area. In addition, fuel suppliers are required to blend
sustainable aviation fuel (SAF) under ReFuelEU Aviation, starting at
2% and rising to 6% by 2030*. SAF still trades at a signiﬁcant
premium. This puts a price on emissions, and in combination, this
adds some $300 per tonne of jet fuel in 2026 (BNEF, ING Research).
Meanwhile, France and the Netherlands have either raised or plan
to raise ticket taxes, particularly on long-haul ﬂying, while Germany

has scaled its levy back and Sweden has abolished it.
High fuel prices put more pressure on low-
cost carriers
*A revision proposal is in progress for aviation, drawing a circle of 5,000 km around
Frankfurt for application of ETS, which would hit Middle East airlines most as they will
then be in scope. **In 2025, the blend rate stood at 2.8%

### Page 10

Low-cost carriers are particularly exposed because fuel accounts
for a larger share of their cost base, and proﬁt bases are more
vulnerable. High hedge ratios initially cushion volatility, but these
hedges gradually roll oﬀ. The more challenging environment is
already visible in the performance of several European low-cost
carriers and will remain an important theme in 2027 if fuel prices
stay elevated.
Premium carriers have more room to manoeuvre. Demand for

premium seats (business, premium economy) is less price-
sensitive, providing airlines with greater scope to pass on higher
costs. Both United Airlines and Delta Airlines reported little impact
after price increases of 15-20% in the second to third quarters.
Passengers are booking later, but airlines continue to indicate a
strong propensity to ﬂy, especially in premium and premium-
economy classes. A minority also accounts for a large share of
ﬂights. In the Netherlands, for example, 25% of people account for

75% of journeys. These frequent travellers tend to be less price-
sensitive.
There has therefore been some demand destruction wiping out part
of the underlying growth. So far, though, the propensity to travel
has remained resilient.
Aviation remains proﬁtable, but margins hit
a post-pandemic low
The fuel shock has signiﬁcantly aﬀected margins. On average,
proﬁts in the global industry are expected to drop meaningfully
below 5%. Airlines including Ryanair, Southwest Airlines and AirAsia

have scaled back capacity on unproﬁtable routes as elevated fuel
prices persisted longer than expected.
According to global aviation consultancy Cirium, around 1% of
global air travel capacity will be removed heading into the Northern
Hemisphere winter of 2026-27. In less mature markets, maintaining
volumes while passing on the higher fuel bill is more diﬃcult.
Airlines may also prioritise less fuel-eﬃcient, older-generation
aircraft over capacity growth.

### Page 11

Industry headwinds could become a catalyst for further
consolidation, ranging from minority stakes to full takeovers.
Fares could retreat if fuel prices ease, but
don't expect too much
Dynamic and algorithm-based pricing is becoming increasingly
common. This helps airlines optimise already historically high load
factors (84% in 2026 so far) but can also contribute to greater fare
volatility, especially as passengers book later amid uncertainty.
If fuel prices ease in 2027, fares could drop back. Yet relatively

strong demand, constrained aircraft availability and general
inﬂation mean a return to early-2026 fare levels looks unlikely.
Cargo beneﬁts from high-tech demand and
supply-chain volatility
Air cargo continues to perform well as a fast transport option in an
increasingly volatile and less predictable world. Volume growth
remained almost 5% year-to-date, while freight rates remained
attractive for carriers.
While e-commerce drove much of the growth in recent years,

digitalisation and large-scale AI investment increasingly support
shipments of high-value technology products. Strong cargo
performance helped cushion pressure on airline results in 2026.
European carriers also beneﬁted from reduced available freight
capacity from Middle Eastern competitors.
Freighter capacity received a boost during the pandemic through
passenger-to-freighter conversions, but that boost has largely run
its course. New-generation A350 and Boeing 777 freighters should

add capacity in the coming years.
Aircargo volume resilient in disruptive
geopolitical environment

Future demand 2027 and onwards
Aviation’s centre of gravity is increasingly
shifting eastwards
The centre of gravity of global aviation is increasingly shifting
eastwards. Trips per capita are expected to see strong increases
over the next two decades, particularly in Asia. This matters even
more because these markets are far more populous.
Until recently, around 90% of the world’s population did not ﬂy in a
given year, but this is gradually changing. As a result, ﬂeet growth is

expected to be strongest in Asia, with its share of the global aircraft
ﬂeet increasing from around 33% to approximately 45%.
People still expected to ﬂy more, especially
in India
Trips per capita per year in various countries

### Page 13

Source: S&P, Airbus, ING Research
India is becoming a powerhouse for future
airline growth
Air travel is strongly correlated with GDP and household income. As
Asia’s middle class expands, the potential customer base for air
travel swells. For future aviation demand, this income eﬀect is even
more important than population growth alone.
Younger generations also appear keen to travel, adding to the
potential in countries such as India and Indonesia. Increasingly

internationally dispersed families provide another structural source
of demand.
India stands out here. Strong economic growth, rising household
incomes and a huge population oﬀer strong potential. Airlines are
positioning themselves accordingly, illustrated by historically large
aircraft orders from IndiGo and Air India. India’s aircraft ﬂeet is
expected to grow by around 8% annually on average over the next
two decades.
China and India are the growth engines of

global aviation in the decades ahead

### Page 14

Source: S&P Global, Airbus, ING Research
Supply-side developments
Aircraft scarcity keeps capacity tight and
lease rates elevated
Supply constraints have been curbing aviation growth since the
post-pandemic recovery and remain a major factor. The share of
stored aircraft dropped below 10% by the end of 2025, its lowest
level since 2019. Even several A380s (the largest aircraft) have
returned to service.
More than half of the global commercial aircraft ﬂeet is leased.

Tight capacity is consequently reﬂected in relatively high used-
aircraft values and lease rates.
Airbus and Boeing had a combined record backlog of around
15,000 aircraft by mid-2026. Production and deliveries continue to
lag order intake after seven consecutive years of subdued deliveries
from 2019 onwards. Demand for new-generation aircraft remains
strong, as illustrated by major recent orders from Avolon and
Turkish Airlines.
Production could ﬁnally return to pre-pandemic levels in 2027.

Chinese manufacturer COMAC, meanwhile, is making progress in
China in Asia, but Western certiﬁcation remains distant.

### Page 15

New airplane deliveries continue on a
recovery track now with Boeing rebounding
Number of delivered commercial airplanes by manufacturers Airbus &
Boeing per year
Source: Company reports *Company forecast
Why aircraft supply remains constrained
Production has still not caught up with pre-pandemic levels
and is unlikely to do so in the next ﬁve years. Aircraft
manufacturing is recovering, but delays and large backlogs
push delivery dates into the 2030s. Airlines are therefore forced

to keep older aircraft ﬂying for longer.
Suppliers are still struggling to ramp up. Six years after the
sharp pandemic downturn, aviation supply chains have still
not fully normalised. Stringent safety and certiﬁcation
requirements mean manufacturers rely on a limited number of
specialised suppliers. Engines and sophisticated cabin
components still have long lead times, while skilled workers
remain scarce.
Engine issues continue to aﬀect available capacity. The peak

impact of the Pratt & Whitney inspection programme after an
identiﬁed production issue has passed, but checks and
maintenance continue to constrain parts of the A320neo-
family ﬂeet and absorb scarce maintenance capacity.

Aircraft production still has a long way to go

Orderbook Airbus & Boeing: number of commercial airplanes
Source: Company reports
Delivery catch-up is crucial for both
capacity and efﬁciency
New aircraft are critical not only to accommodate growth, but also
to improve eﬃciency. The average age of the global ﬂeet has
increased from around 13 to 15 years since 2019 due to production
constraints.
This matters increasingly as older units require more maintenance

at a time when skilled personnel and maintenance capacity are
scarce; they also consume signiﬁcantly more fuel and therefore
emit more.
Boeing is ﬁnally regaining altitude
supporting deliveries
Boeing is ﬁnally making a sustained comeback following an
unprecedented period of disruption stemming from the 737 MAX
accidents, the pandemic and subsequent production and quality
problems. It received certiﬁcation for its 737 MAX 7 and also the
737 MAX 10 recently. Boeing plans to increase production for the

family from 38 to 47 a month.

### Page 17

Higher 737 production should support deliveries in 2027 and
gradually bring pre-pandemic production levels back into sight. This
is hugely important for Boeing-dependent airlines such as Ryanair
and Southwest. Progress on certiﬁcation of the 777-9 is also
important for long-haul ﬂeet renewal.
Fleet renewal is currently the most
important decarbonisation lever
Air travel continues to grow, and associated emissions tend to
continue to rise further as well. Sustainable aviation fuels are

labelled as an important industry pathway to decarbonise, but
globally, blend rates still hover below 1% in 2026.
The closure of airspaces, too, has created ineﬃciencies and extra
emissions over the last few years, undermining gains elsewhere.
Around 63% of the global ﬂeet still consists of previous-generation
aircraft. Replacing them can reduce fuel consumption by 10-25% or
more, making ﬂeet renewal one of the most eﬀective near-term
decarbonisation levers available to airlines.

The challenge is to deliver proﬁtable growth
in a more disruptive world
For airlines, the challenge heading into 2027 is therefore not simply

ﬁnding suﬃcient demand. Relatively poor punctuality, with only
around 61% of ﬂights departing on time in 2026 year-to-date, is a
signal.
The globally operating industry faces more turbulence and
uncertainty amid intensiﬁed geopolitical risks. The challenge is to
deliver proﬁtable growth in a world of persistently constrained
capacity, higher costs and more frequent disruption.

Oct Edition: Choppy Markets, Intact AI

Overseas  
Technology

Jeff Pu, CFA 
SFC CE No. BNO719

Henry Huang 
SFC CE No. BYA749 
henryhuang@gfgroup.com.hk

Market to remain choppy: Tech equities were volatile in the past month, with rotation 
toward hyperscaler and internet names. That said , s emi and memory (Korea) 
weakness reflected lower risk appetite due to rising bond yields, AI monetization/ARR 
visibility and L-T AI debates (i.e., Broadcom CEO warned that capital may now be a 
bigger constraint), even as company-specific catalysts continued to be strong. On the 
other hand, the positive was the acceleration of AI model scaling (i.e., Meta’s Muse 
Wonder, Gemini 4, etc). That said, w e view the pullback primarily as a reset in 
expectations rather than the evidence of AI deterioration. Despite light positioning, we 
expect the markets will remain choppy in the near term but would  accumulate low 
valuation & sustainable names  (i.e., HBM, non-TSMC foundries, CPUs, CPOs, etc) . 
In our preferred list, we added CLS, while removing MTK and Hon Hai.  
U.S. roadshow takeaways (week of Oct 5): Investor tone skewed neutral to slightly 
cautious, driven by conservative top ‑down macro views, U.S. election, and ongoing 
debates around the L-T AI demand. Positioning in AI infrastructure has come off peak 
levels. Even  so, many investors would accumulate on pullbacks, with interest 
concentrated in “Made in U.S.” names (INTC, LITE, COHR), U.S. memory suppliers, 
TSMC, and selective bottleneck themes — including ASE, ABF substrates, 
MRVL/SMTC, and Japanese MLCCs. 
OCP & earnings season in focus: The week is anchored by AI/semis — SEMICON 
West, OCP, and earnings from ASML, TSMC, etc. OCP reinforced the industry’s pivot 
toward rack‑scale infrastructure, with inference and agentic workloads driving demand 
across compute, memory, and networking. Scale‑up optics, NPO/CPO, and 800VDC 
power were key themes, while AMD’s CPU outlook and Helios/Helion underscored a 
secular CPU uptrend. Nvidia ’s use of PTFE for NVLink boards further supports our 
positive stance on PTFE (see PCB Spec in 2 H27, Oct 5).  On earnings, ASML 
delivered largely in -line results, GM and OP above consensus with revenue slightly 
miss, and raised full year guidance again and is exploring ways to increase capacity 
by >30% 2028 to fulfill strong customer demand, also extending the order visibility 
beyond 2028. For TSMC, we anticipate an inline quarter. 
September monthly sales/data: TSMC sales -0.6% MoM, slightly ahead. Rubin to 
accelerate and T3 ramp. PC forecast trimmed. Substrate/CCL upcycle with 
pricing/margin expansion. Aspeed revenue +101% YoY. Details on page 5. 
GFHK Tech Most Read:  1) Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9 ; 2) Michelle Jing 
on PCB Spec in 2H27; 3) Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration; 4) Jeff Pu on NAND Re-
acceleration; 5) Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview.

### Page 3

Sector Report | Technology

GFHK Tech Important Stock Calls  
In the past one month, our key reports and stock calls are: 
Jeff Pu on ASIC Updates on TPU v9, reported date on Oct 13: We expect near-term MTK headwinds 
from TPU v9 delays but remain constructive on MTK/MRVL longer term. Humufish’s redesign could 
push tape-out to Jan -27, MP tape -out to 2Q27 and shipments to 1Q28, while EMIB -T yields could 
recover from 45–50%. Rising TPU allocations to Anthropic could constrain Gemini compute, though 
higher internal allocation in 2H26 may support future models. SemiAnalysis expects Anthropic/OpenAI 
to each add 5 –10GW in 2027 vs. a few GW for Google. For TPU v10 (4Q28 /1Q29), MTK/GUC’s 
Icefish remains the only confirmed mainstream SKU, while Broadcom-led Merope faces AMD/Marvell 
competition, with both potentially testing EMIB-T. 
Michelle Jing on PCB Spec in 2H27, reported date on Oct 5: Some market watchers expect Nvidia 
to shift from PTFE to hydrocarbon resin (HC) for the VR300 NVL576 switch tray. HC—one of the glass 
free alternatives within PTFE hybrid architectures—has only recently passed SI testing and has yet to 
enter PCB MP, leaving it roughly three months behind PTFE in qualification. On the contrary, we 
believe that PTFE offers superior performance for high frequency, high speed designs. With VR300 
NVL576 shipments expected in 2H27 and the final configuration likely set by end 26 —and with a 
leading Chinese PCB ma ker already achieving meaningful PTFE hybrid yields —we maintain that 
PTFE hybrid remains the most probable solution for next year’s switch board. 
Alicia Xia on CPO Acceleration , reported date on  Sept 29 : Nvidia’s CPO scale -out switch is 
progressing faster than expected, with FAU yields improving. We forecast CPO switch shipments at 
15k/16k/30k in 4Q26/1Q27/2Q27 and 100k in 2027. For Nvidia/AWS scale -up, CPO and NPO 
continue in parallel, with full NPO adoption expected at NVL576 launch. Feynman is also devel oping 
both variants, with CPO potentially using QM6 + six 6.4T OEs. We shift AWS Trainium 4 launch to 
1Q28 and forecast global scale-up OE demand at 6.5m/42m in 2027E/28E. 
Jeff Pu on NAND Re -acceleration, reported date on Sept 20 : According to TrendForce, NAND 
demand is stronger than expected as U.S. CSPs scale AI infrastructure, with eSSD demand nearly 
2x prior forecasts. We raise NAND contract price growth to +21%/+17%/+15% QoQ in 
3Q26/4Q26/1Q27, while hyperscaler inventories decline. Supply remains disci plined at 15%/21%/20% 
YoY bit growth in 2025 –27. Demand is supported by agentic AI, vector databases and KV -cache 
offloading, where eSSD offers lower cost/bit than DRAM and better power efficiency than HDD. 
Jeff Pu on TSMC 3Q26 Preview , reported date on  Oct 7 : TSMC shares hit record highs on 
expectations ahead of the print and potential Terafab collaboration. We expect largely inline 3Q26 
results, capex and full-year guidance, with softer 4Q26 margin (67.2%). Management should reaffirm 
2026 capex at the ceiling and mid-40s YoY growth, while we forecast 37% growth in 2027 on sustained 
AI/CPU/networking demand and price hikes. Key focuses include margins, long -term capex/U.S. 
expansion, large-reticle packaging and A14. We raise 2026E/27E EPS by 2%/8% on hig her N2/N3 
capacity and operating leverage, and roll to 20x 2027E P/E, lifting TP to NT$2,960.

September Monthly Data  
TSMC September sales of NT$511.9bn, -0.6% MoM and +54.6% YoY, with total 3Q revenue reaching 
NT$1.49T, +17.6 QoQ, slightly ahead of expectations of +14%. The solid performance was well 
supported by ASIC, A20 Pro, and Rubin. For 3Q26, we expect gross margin to be 67.8%, higher than 
guidance of 65-67% and consensus of 66.4% . For 4Q26, we expect another 12% QoQ growth with 
GM at 67.2%, both ahead of street’s estimates of +10% QoQ and 66% margin. Ahead of the earnings 
call, key focus include gross margin trajectory, long -term capex discipline, and the execution of its 
U.S. fab expansion. 
Rubin to accelerate:  Hon Hai revenue last month was NT$1.16T, +26 % MoM, +38% YoY, mainly 
driven by iPhone build.  Quanta and Wistron delivered -23% and -17% MoM respectively, due to AI 
racks’ shipping timing differences. Of note, Pegatron surprised the market with +143.2% MoM revenue 
surge fueled by initial GB300 rack shipments to neocloud customers including Lambda and Together 
AI. This reaffirms our thesis of robust NVL72 order momentum across Neoclouds. Looking ahead, 
VR200 is ramping now, with volume deployment accelerating into 2027. We maintain our forecast of 
75k and 92k NVL72 (GB+VR) rack shipments for 2026 and 2027, respectively. Concurrently, Wiwynn 
delivered another solid month with revenue expanding 20.0% MoM, underpinne d by resilient GP 
server demand alongside the ramp of Trainium 3. 
ASIC ramping up: On design service vendors fronts, driven  by T3 ramp, Alchip’s Sep revenue 
reached a record NT$9.9 8bn. Backed by secured incremental 3nm wafer allocation and improving 
yields, management raised its 2027 revenue guidance, now targeting growth above the previous 50% 
YoY baseline. That said, we push out T4 shipment timeline into 1Q28. GUC delivered a strong beat 
with monthly revenue reaching a record NT$7.145bn (+22.4% MoM, +97.8 % YoY), driven by robust 
Axion 2 CPU demand. Beyond its engagement with top 4 CSPs, multi -year order visibility into 2027 
and beyond is further bolstered by high-margin (likely TK1) automotive ASIC designs. 
PC forecast trimmed: NB shipments from top 5 ODMs were 9.7m in Sep, with 3Q26 shipments down 
17% QoQ and 26% YoY. Global PC OEM shipments contracted 20.1% YoY in 3Q26, worse than 
expected, post robust 1H26 pull -ins with high pricing points and weak macro. For 2027, we project 
the industry will decline a further 5% YoY. On the competitive front, tier -1 vendors such as Lenovo, 
Dell, and HP are not immune to this downturn, while entry-level laptops (low cost models from China) 
continue to see stronger relative demand.  
Substrate/CCL Upcycle Intact: Unimicron continued to deliver another all-time high sales with +5% 
MoM and 65% YoY. Supported by ABF upward pricing of ~15% in 2H26 (likely more next year) and 
higher mix of AI products, we expect a meaningful GM uptick for this quarter with momentum to sustain 
into 2027 . In the meantime, ZengDing’s revenue rose 28% MoM and 36% YoY mainly driven by  
iPhone, 1.6T mSAP, and Rubin’s HDI. On the CCL front, bolstered by ongoing price hikes and strong 
demand from GP servers, ITEC revenue grew 15.5% MoM and 111% YoY, and NanYa Plastics 
recorded +12.5% MoM and +55% YoY. Meanwhile, EMC and TUC both posted another single digit 
growth MoM on sales.

Sector Report | Technology

Downstream tech – Smartphone & Apple  
We maintain Global smartphone forecast of -14% in 2026  
According to IDC, the preliminary 2Q26 smartphone data points to a still ‑soft market with uneven 
regional dynamics, and their commentary continues to emphasize structural rather than cyclical 
headwinds. Smartphone shipments remain under pressure from weak replacement cycles, cautious 
consumer spending, and limited breakthrough features. IDC typically highlights that “growth is 
concentrated in the premium tier,” driven by ecosystem lock ‑in, while the mass market remains 
challenged by price sensitivity. They also tend to note that China and parts of emerging markets show 
only modest stabilization, not a full recovery, and that vendors are prioritizing margin discipline over 
volume.  
Overall, by factoring in higher iPhone 17/17 Pro volume, we fine tune our global smartphone shipment 
forecast to -14% YoY from -15% YoY. 
For 2027, we expect global smartphone shipments to be flattish YoY. That said, 2026 -2027 will be 
one of the most challenging periods for the industry, driven primarily by sharply higher memory prices, 
which have already pushed OEMs to reduce shipments and raise device prices.

Sector Report | Technology

iPhone 18 Pro/18 PM demand lukewarm  
iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max wait-time lukewarm: iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone 18 Pro Max pre-orders began 
on Sept 12 in the U.S. and many other countries. Generally speaking, the wait-time appears lukewarm, 
with: 1) 3–4 weeks for 256GB/512GB Pro Max across all colors, as well as Burgundy 1TB in the U.S.；
2) 2–3 weeks for Glacier 256GB/512GB Pro Max in China; 3) 1–2 weeks for Black/Glacier 1TB Pro 
Max in the U.S. ；4) immediate availability for Silver 1TB and mos t 2TB Pro Max models in the U.S, 
as well as most iPhone 18 Pro configurations except for Burgundy 256GB in the U.S. We attribute the 
lukewarm wait-time to limited spec upgrade, higher pricing (especially for large storage models), etc. 
We also expect the Pro Max to be impacted by iPhone Duo and the criticism of heavy weight.  
iPhone Duo feedback generally positive 
The iPhone Duo, priced at $1,999 in the U.S., opened pre‑orders on Oct 16 and officially launched on 
Oct 23. Early reviews were positive, highlig hting its polished folding experience, high ‑quality inner 
display, and improved multitasking in iOS 27, but also point to trade ‑offs including a heavier and 
thicker form factor, a visible crease, and a dual ‑camera. Due to hinge constraints, we are reducing  
the iPhone Duo EMS build plan from 7m to 6m units in 2026. We also lower the iPhone 18 Pro/Pro 
Max build plan to 72m units given the constraints associated with the adjustable‑aperture camera.

### Page 14

Sector Report | Technology

Downstream tech – PC 
Global PC units down to -12% in 2026, followed by another decline in 2027 
On Oct 8, IDC reported 3Q26 global PC shipments fell 20.1% YoY, missing street expectations of a 
10–15% decline. The sharper contraction was primarily driven by front -loaded demand in 1H26, 
elevated ASPs, and broader macro weakness. This aligns with our prior view that ODM build 
momentum moderated starting in end -2Q26, amid the rising concerns over channel inventory. 
Meanwhile, the top 5 ODMs also saw 3Q26 shipments decline 26% YoY. Looking ahead, we model 
4Q26 shipments to continue 20% YoY drop (flattish QoQ), underperforming normal seasonality. 
On the component front, we expect memory de -spec to normalize. End -users still require sufficient 
DRAM capacity (16GB+) to handle on -device AI workloads, and PC DDR 5 supply constraints have 
eased compared to Server’s. On pricing, new models will still have higher pricing points to pass 
through 2H component cost increases across memory, CPU, etc. That said, we anticipate price hikes 
will remain measured vs 1H26 given sluggish end-market demand. 
Overall, reflecting weaker than expected 3Q26 results post pull -ins and a muted 4Q outlook, we trim 
our 2026 PC shipment forecast to -12% YoY. For 2027, we project shipments to decline further by 5%

U.S. CSP capex to be +85%/+53%/+20% in 2026/2027/2028 
Given capex expands further into 2027, CSPs free cash flow are undergoing significant pressure and 
turning negative except Microsoft, such that shifting to debt financing for their massive capex. In 2025, 
Top 5 CSPs’ total debt issuance reached $108bn, or 24% of total capex . Per our Addressing 2028 
Capex and AI Platform Value Shift report on Aug 6, we forecast Top 5 CSPs’ debt issuance to reach 
$257bn/$419bn in 2026/2027, or 31%/35% of total capex.  Assuming debt issuance to grow in 
2028/2029 at a mild pace, we believe U.S. CSPs' balance sheet (except Oracle) to remain very healthy, 
due to net leverage (EBITDA basis) of only 0.5 and the net debt-to-equity ratio of only 18% by 2029.  
Despite healthy balance sheet for most CSPs, we expect most to safeguard balance sheets beyond 
2027 amid widening spreads and FCF profiles, with the focus shifting to returns. Together with a swing 
factor of competition from Chinese LLMs, we expect U.S. CSPs to decelerate capex expansion. We 
forecast Top 5 CSPs capex of $1,512bn/1,739bn in 2028/2029, or +20%/15% YoY, down from +53% 
in 2027.

Upstream tech – Optical 
Strong demand for pluggable through 2028. 
We expect that the total demand for 800G/1.6T will reach 90mn/80mn in 2027, increased by 
80%/167%+, mainly driven by: 1) Nvidia, Google and AWS and other ASIC’s Accelerators’ demand 
are ramping up; 2) GPU/ASIC scale -out bandwidth continuously  increase drives increasing 
GPU/ASIC-to-optical module ratio; 3) We expect that TPU for 2027 will be 8.9m and Google fully 
transitions to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up layer will adopt optical interconnects. In 
addition, as we expect that CPO/NPO will start since 2H27, represents a pure incremental opportunity 
for optical interconnects supply chain, benefiting FAU, laser, shuffle box, connector and OE players, 
including LITE, GLW, TSEM, Sumitomo and Browave. We also like Marvell and SMTC, for the 
TIA/Driver used in NPO OE.  
Scale-out bandwidth increase driving optical-module deployment 
For NVIDIA, each Rubin GPU is equipped with two CX9 NIC chips, doubling the scale-out bandwidth 
compared with Blackwell. We further estimate that Rubin Ultra will adopt four CX9 chips per GPU, 
leading to another 2 times increase in scale-out bandwidth, continuing NVIDIA’s consistent bandwidth-
doubling trajectory. Accordingly, we expect the ratio of 1.6T optical modules has risen from 1:2.5 to 
1:5 in Rubin. From CoWoS perspective, we expect 5m/2m Blackwell/Rubin and 20m 1.6T in 2026. 
For Google, we believe that Goo gle fully transition to large -scale clusters in 2026 and the scale -up 
layer will adopt optical interconnects. We believe that in the scale-up domain, the ratio of TPU to 1.6T 
optical modules is approximately 1:1.5; In the scale-out domain, the ratio is around 1:2.5, suggesting 
the overall TPU-to-optics ratio is approximately 1:4 (1.6T equivalent).

Nvidia/CSPs’ Scale-out/up CPO/NPO  
Within NVIDIA’s next -gen NVL576 and broader CSPs’ AI infrastructure plans, CPO and NPO are 
developed side -by-side to expand GP U/ASIC interconnect bandwidth: CPO is the ideal long -term 
design for lower power and latency yet faces headwinds from complicated advanced packaging, and 
yield risks, while NPO could deliver most short -reach optical performance gains with far better 
manufacturability and deployment flexibility, making NPO a reliable near -term scaling path and CPO 
the ultimate end -state architecture. NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal 
conditioning and equalization to system designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that 
take over part of traditional DSP functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens of 
dollars worth of combined TIA and driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics. 
Our shipment forecasts at 6.5m/42.4m NPO optical engines in CY27/28.  
Scale-up domain to increase by NVL576 to compensate lower memory: With lower memory spec, we 
expect Nvidia to increase the scale -up domain by optics to enhance performance. According to 
Nvidia’s GTC 2026, th e optical scale -up fabric allows Nvidia to expand Rubin Ultra to an NVL576 
scale-up. Regarding CPO and NPO which are being parallelly developed, we expect NPO to be a 
high reliability scaling path for NPO. We now expect its scale -up to adopt NPO in Rubin U ltra and 
Feynman platform and estimate OE shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028.

### Page 25

Sector Report | Technology

Nvidia’s NPO OE to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028 
For Nvidia, we now expect its scale up to adopt NPO in Rubin Ultra and Feynman platform and 
estimate OE  shipments of Nvidia’s platform to be 6.0m/19.7m in 2027/2028, based on full NPO 
adoption at NVL576 launch given NPO maturity. Coupled with other CSP’s platform, we estimate total 
OE market to be 6.5m/42.4m in 2027/2028.  
TIA/Driver to benefit from NPO 
NPO partially removes module -side DSP and shifts signal conditioning and equalization to system 
designs, greatly lifting the value of TIAs and laser drivers that take over part of traditional DSP 
functions; we estimate each 3.2T optical engine contains tens o f dollars worth of combined TIA and 
driver chips, a clear improvement over standard pluggable optics.  
A key architectural shift under NPO is the partial removal of DSP functionality at the module level, with 
signal conditioning and equalization increasing ly redistributed into system -level design. This 
significantly elevates the importance of TIA (transimpedance amplifier) and laser driver components, 
which effectively replace a portion of traditional DSP value. We estimate that each 3.2T optical engine 
will embed high tens of dollars of combined TIA and driver content, representing a meaningful step -
up versus traditional pluggable optics architectures.  
From a competitive standpoint, we expect Marvell to remain the primary beneficiary of both NPO OE 
given i ts stronger positioning high speed TIA/Driver. Semtech is well positioned as a secondary 
beneficiary through its exposure to high-speed analog signal chain components, particularly TIAs and 
laser drivers, which scale directly with optical engine proliferat ion under NPO. We forecast 
6.5mn/42.4mn NPO OE shipments in CY27/28. Based on our estimated front-end content per OE and 
a 30% market share assumption for Semtech, we estimate its NPO revenue of $315/936mn. 
Stock Implication 
Generally speaking, scale-up CPO/NPO is an incremental opportunity for optical interconnect supply 
chain, as it aims to replace copper and has no impact on scale -out side. Additionally, scale up and 
scale out CPO solution share the similar suppliers. That said, we expect the key benefic iaries to be 
components such as FAU, CW laser, ELSFP, shuffle, PIC, TIA and Driver. We: 1) prefer Lumentum/ 
COHR due to the demand upside for CW laser, and also like local cw laser players; 2) expect Browave 
(3163 TT) to benefit, given the sizable contribu tion of CPO’s shuffle box considering ~55% market 
share, $5-6k ASP and a volume ramp in 2H26; 3) we also like TSEM, given CSP preferring NPO 
solution for their ASICs scale up interconnection. 4) expect GLW to benefit, given the second supplier 
of shuffle b ox and main supplier of MMC connector/fiber. 5) Marvell/Semtech will also benefit from 
CSP/Nvidia’s NPO solution, since TIA and driver will upgrade in the NPO Optical Engine.

### Page 28

Sector Report | Technology

Google’s 6D Torus & Beneficiaries.  
We expect Google will migrate its TPU interconnect from 3D to 6D torus, re -organizing the 64 -chip 
elemental block from 4× 4× 4 to 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2. External optical links per block rise from 96 to 384, with 
optical ports per chip scaling increase from 1:1.5 to 1:6, driving 4x coherent lite transceivers and OCS 
demand. The trajectory aligns with NVIDIA's CPO/NPO move. Both are interconnect scaling that 
expands direct chip-to-chip bandwidth and reduces hop count, with TPU via a 6D torus and NVIDIA 
via CPO/NPO for Scale-up. 
Google’s migration from 3D  to 6D Torus: We expect Google to migrate its TPU interconnect from a 
3D to a 6D torus topology, re-organizing the elemental 64-chip building block from a 4× 4× 4 3D Torus 
cube into a 2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2 hyper-cube. In the current 3D design, each chip connects to 6 nearest 
neighbors across X/Y/Z axes and the cube exposes 16 optical ICI links on each of its 6 faces, totaling 
96 external optical links per cube (~1.5 optical ports per chip). Under the 6D design, each dimension 
has 64 external optical links (2⁶ chips, on e outward link per chip per dimension), for a total of 6 ×  64 
= 384 external optical links (~6 optical ports per chip). The per-chip optical port count thus scales from 
1.5 to 6. Combined with Scale -out at an estimated 1:2.5, this brings the aggregate TPU -to-port ratio 
to 1:8.5. 
Lower latency and closer to non -blocking Scale-up: For a 4,096 -chip superpod, a 3D torus (4× 4× 4) 
carries a worst-case diameter of 24 hops, whereas a 6D torus (2× 2× 2× 2× 2× 2) halves that to 12 hops 
(2× 6), directly compressing collective-operation and latency. Higher dimensionality also spreads traffic 
across 6 orthogonal paths, improving bisection bandwidth and moving closer to non-blocking chip-to-
chip communication. Critically, shortening each edge from 4 to 2 nodes eliminates deep mu lti-hop 
electrical paths inside the block: the architecture deliberately reduces intra -cube copper 
communication and pushes a far larger share of traffic onto optical fiber. 
Key beneficiaries: The exact TPU generation for the adoption of 6D torus and initi al penetration rate 
remain unclear. That said, whether NVIDIA moves toward CPO/NPO or Google expands from 3D to 
6D, both are Scale -up strategies aimed at enabling greater inter -chip communication. Key 
beneficiaries include the 2.4T Coherent Lite module and the OCS supply chain, such as MEMS-based 
optical switch vendors, PIC/laser/DSP suppliers, and fiber/cabling vendors supporting the larger 
reconfigurable lightwave fabric.

Risks 
1) Alternative technologies; 2) AI demand and capex slowdown; 3) IT demand slowdown; 4) product 
delays.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting
**Please note that this will be the last Westpac press release for
the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 điểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 điểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao 
phục vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức 
cao cho phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện 
đáng kể trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm 
phán vẫn đang bế tắc và ít có khả năng dòng chảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối 
lại ngay trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi lựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đầu tư công, chính sách tiền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

I. Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
TTCK Việt Nam đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 
trong 9 tháng đầu năm 
 TTCK Việt Nam biến động giằng co, giảm điểm nhẹ trong 9 tháng đầu năm với 
diễn biến có thể chia làm 2 giai đoạn chính. Giai đoạn 1 kéo dài đến gần cuối 
quý 1, thị trường đi ngang trong 2 tháng đầu, trước khi lao dốc mạnh từ cuối 
tháng 2 trước ảnh hưởng bởi xung đột tại Trung Đông bùng phát. Giai đoạn 2 là 
khoảng thời gian còn lại, thị trường xuất hiện các nhịp biến động mạnh, 
tăng/giảm đan xen, chịu ảnh hưởng bởi thông tin về giá dầu, biến động lãi suất 
trong nước, kỳ vọng về nâng hạng thị trường và các yếu tố cơ bản khác. Tính 
chung 9 tháng, VNIndex gần như đi ngang, giảm nhẹ 0.9% so với cuối 2025, 
trong khi thanh khoản bình quân toàn thị trường giảm xấp xỉ 12% so với cùng 
kỳ.

### Page 4
